Our contributions are threefold: (i) we formulate tool retrieval as query-conditioned hyperedge prediction on a tool co-invocation hypergraph, under which the tool set itself becomes the unit of scoring and most existing retrieval paradigms reduce to restricted instances; (ii) we capture size-dependent tool compatibility through cardinality-specific interactions; and (iii) we design HYSET as a pre-selection module requiring no modification to the downstream agent. Experiments on ToolBench demonstrate that HYSET consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both tool retrieval performance and end-to-end task success. Beyond the in-domain setting, HYSET further supports zero-shot/few-shot transfer, generalizing to held-out tools/categories and unseen domains with minimal supervision.

</details>


### [61] [Optimization with Dynamic Constraint Learning (DCL)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25719)
*Ezgi Oztekin,Figen Oztoprak,S. Ilker Birbil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态约束学习（DCL）框架，在约束函数未知且无法查询时，利用局部代理模型和置信域方法求解约束优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际优化问题中约束函数通常未知或难以显式建模，现有全局学习方法需要大量数据且计算成本高，亟需一种高效的数据驱动约束优化方法。

Method: 在每次迭代中，从邻近数据点学习局部代理模型替代真实约束，并在数据支持的置信域内求解子问题；代理模型随优化过程动态调整。

Result: 在合成测试问题和两个文献案例中验证了DCL的有效性，其解质量与全局模型相当，但使用了更简单的局部模型和更小的子问题。

Conclusion: DCL通过局部自适应学习实现高效约束优化，在降低计算复杂度的同时保持接近全局方法的解质量。

Abstract: We propose Dynamic Constraint Learning (DCL), a data-driven framework for constrained optimization when constraint functions are unknown and cannot be queried during optimization. At each iteration, the method learns a local surrogate from nearby data and solves a subproblem within a data-supported trust region. Compared with offline global constraint learning, the approach uses local surrogates that adapt to the data distribution during optimization and can achieve solution quality comparable to that of global models while using simpler local models and smaller optimization subproblems. We demonstrate the performance of DCL on a synthetic test problem and two case studies from the literature.

</details>


### [62] [Detecting CSAM Text-to-Image LoRAs From Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25750)
*David Demitri Africa,Cate Heine,Nadine Staes-Polet,Kimberly Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于权重分析的方法，通过LoRA更新矩阵的左上奇异向量（u₁）形成无推理的指纹，用于识别有害LoRA（如用于生成CSAM的LoRA），无需依赖元数据或生成输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LoRA微调模型审核依赖元数据或生成输出，但元数据可被篡改，生成输出本身可能违法或不可接受，因此需要一种更安全的审核信号。

Method: 提取LoRA更新矩阵的左上奇异向量（u₁）作为指纹，以人类年龄为良性代理，分析该向量能否识别LoRA的训练内容、是否可跨基模型泛化、能否区分有害与无关内容，并测试其对权重噪声、缩放和精度降低的鲁棒性。

Result: u₁指纹能有效识别LoRA的训练内容，可跨基模型泛化，对无关良性内容可放弃判断，且对加性噪声、缩放和精度降低均具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 有害LoRA可直接通过权重筛查，无需依赖元数据或生成有害输出，为模型微调的安全审核提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning has made it cheap and easy to customize open-weight image generation models for specific tasks, including the production of child sexual abuse material (CSAM). Existing moderation relies on metadata or generated outputs, but metadata can be deceptive and generating outputs may itself be unacceptable or illegal. We show that a safer signal lives in the weights. The top-left singular vectors of a LoRA's updates form a compact, inference-free fingerprint ($u_1$) of its strongest learned change. Using human-subject age as a benign proxy for CSAM, we find that $u_1$ identifies what a LoRA was trained on, generalizes across base models, and abstains on unrelated benign content. The signal is robust to additive weight noise, rescaling, and precision reduction. These results indicate that harmful LoRAs could be screened directly from their weights without relying on metadata or generating harmful outputs.

</details>


### [63] [An Embarrassingly Simple Rule-based Visiting Circulation Approach to Trip Destination Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25751)
*Eng-Shen Tu,Yong-Han Chen,En-Chao Liu,Hao-Yun Keng,Cheng-Te Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对IEEE Big Data Cup 2022中的目的地预测挑战，提出了一种基于规则的访问循环（RVC）模型。该模型无需从训练区域学习，直接利用出发地信息和个体出行行为来预测目标大都市区的目的地，在离线评估和排行榜提交中均显著优于监督学习方法和其他启发式方法，最终获得比赛第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督学习方法无法解决目标大都市区目的地完全未知的挑战，需要一种无需依赖训练区域标记数据的方法来预测目的地。

Method: 提出基于规则的访问循环（RVC）模型，直接利用出发地信息和个体出行行为中的重复访问模式以及出发地-目的地关系，通过规则而非学习的方式确定目标大都市区的目的地。

Result: 在离线评估和排行榜提交中，RVC显著优于监督学习方法和其他启发式方法，最终在比赛中获得第二名。

Conclusion: 基于规则的RVC模型能够有效解决目标区域目的地完全未知的跨区域目的地预测问题，证明了重复访问行为和出发地-目的地关系在个体出行中的关键作用。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Rule-based Visiting Circulation (RVC) model in tackling the challenge in the IEEE Big Data Cup 2022: Trip Destination Prediction. Given trips containing travel information, personal attributes, origin zones, and their features in the training metropolitan areas, the task is to predict the destination of every trip in a targeted metropolitan area whose destinations are not given at all at the training stage. We highlight the challenges in this destination prediction task -- having no knowledge of the destinations in the targeted metropolitan area. We provide insights from the datasets, in which revisiting behaviors and the relationships between origins and destinations play a crucial role in individuals' trips. Hence, we design a simple but comprehensive method, rule-based visiting circulation, which directly utilizes the origin information and individuals' trip behaviors to determine the destinations in the targeted metropolitan area, i.e., requiring no learning from the four training areas. Experimental results on both offline evaluation and leaderboard submission consistently exhibit the proposed RVC can significantly outperform supervised learning methods and other heuristics. The RVC method eventually brings us to second place in the competition leaderboard.

</details>


### [64] [SpectONet: A Physics-Guided Spectral Deep Operator Network for Euler-Bernoulli Beam Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25790)
*Shivani Saini,Ramesh Kumar Vats,Arup Kumar Sahoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpectONet，一种将DeepONet与物理约束及切比雪夫-高斯-洛巴托（CGL）非均匀传感器布局相结合的算子学习框架，用于求解欧拉-伯努利梁振动问题。相比传统均匀布点，CGL在边界附近密集采样，以更少的输入点高效表征边界敏感的结构响应。物理方程与初边值条件嵌入损失函数。在三个合成EBB问题和一个真实桥梁振动数据集上，相比Vanilla DeepONet、PI-DeepONet、PINN和CNN-UNet，SpectONet在合成问题上至少提升64%，在真实问题上至少提升37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DeepONet使用均匀传感器分布，在处理边界敏感的梁振动问题时需要大量输入点才能捕捉边界附近的高频或突变响应，导致计算效率低且边界表征能力不足。现有方法（如PINN、PI-DeepONet）虽引入物理约束，但未专门优化传感器采样策略以适配结构动力学问题的边界敏感性。

Method: SpectONet将DeepONet的算子学习能力与物理信息约束结合，采用切比雪夫-高斯-洛巴托（CGL）非均匀传感器布局——在边界附近密集采样、内部稀疏采样，以少量分支网络输入获得高质量边界表征。将Euler-Bernoulli梁控制方程、初始条件与边界条件作为损失函数项，强制预测满足物理一致性。

Result: 在三个合成EBB振动问题和一个真实桥梁振动数据集上，SpectONet在预测误差上全面优于Vanilla DeepONet、PI-DeepONet、PINN和CNN-UNet。合成问题相比基线至少提升64%，真实问题至少提升37%。

Conclusion: SpectONet提供了一种精确、计算高效且物理一致的算子学习框架，适用于结构振动分析，尤其适用于边界敏感的结构动力学问题。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel physics-guided spectral deep operator network, termed SpectONet, for solving Euler-Bernoulli beam (EBB) vibration problems. The proposed framework integrates the operator-learning capability of DeepONet with physics-informed constraints and Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto (CGL) sensor placement. Unlike conventional DeepONet frameworks, which commonly employ uniformly distributed sensors, SpectONet uses nonuniform spectral sensor locations with a higher concentration of points near the domain boundaries. This sampling strategy improves the finite-dimensional representation of boundary-sensitive structural responses while requiring only a limited number of branch-network inputs. The governing beam equation, together with the associated initial and boundary conditions, incorporated into the training objective to promote physically consistent and generalizable predictions. Numerical experiments on three synthetic EBB vibration problems and a real-world bridge vibration dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. Comparisons with strong baselines such as, Vanilla DeepONet, PI-DeepONet, PINN, and CNN-UNet show that SpectONet consistently achieves lower prediction errors across all considered evaluation metrics. In particular, SpectONet achieves at least \(64\%\) improvement over the considered baseline models across the three synthetic problems and at least \(37\%\) for the real-world problems. These results demonstrate that SpectONet provides an accurate, computationally efficient, and physically consistent operator-learning framework for structural vibration analysis.

</details>


### [65] [Prototype Adaptation for Zero-Shot sEMG Movement Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25826)
*Rui Liu,Benjamin Paassen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CPI和SAP两种方法，在仅用基础动作训练的情况下，实现对手部组合动作的零样本识别，精度提升超过20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sEMG假肢控制研究多聚焦于基础动作识别，但日常活动中组合动作至关重要。收集所有可能组合动作的训练数据耗时且需重新训练模型，因此需要一种无需针对组合动作重新训练就能识别新组合动作的方法。

Method: 提出Compositional Prototype Interpolation (CPI) 和 Synthetic Adaptation for Prototypes (SAP)两种方法，基于嵌入空间中的线性插值假设，利用原型网络在仅训练基础动作后实现组合动作的零样本学习。

Result: 在NearLab、NinaPro DB3和新录制的BasCom数据集上，SAP方法在组合动作识别上相比已有零样本学习方法准确率提升超过20%，在线推理实验中也保持优势。

Conclusion: 通过线性插值假设和原型网络，可在无需组合动作训练数据的情况下有效识别未见过的组合手部动作，显著提升假肢控制的实用性和灵活性。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) enables the control of prostheses, allowing upper-limb amputees to re-gain some hand function. Most current research focuses on recognizing basic movements for prosthesis control. However, in most daily activities, such as opening a door, combined movements are essential. However, collecting training data for all possible combined movements is time-consuming and requires re-training of the model for any new combination. We propose two novel recognition approaches, Compositional Prototype Interpolation (CPI) and Synthetic Adaptation for Prototypes (SAP), that enable zero-shot learning of combined, novel and unseen movements in Prototype Networks after training only with basic movements. Our methods rest on a linear interpolation assumption in the embedding space, which we study by inspecting the geometry of combined motions in signal and embedding space. In experiments on the NearLab and NinaPro DB3 data sets as well as our newly recorded BasCom dataset, our proposed SAP outperforms prior zero-shot learning methods with accuracy improvements on combined movements of more than 20%. This advantage is maintained in online inference experiments in a user study.

</details>


### [66] [DRIFT: Direct-Recursive Intervention-Conditioned Forecasting of ICU Physiological Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25864)
*Weixin Liu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Yanfan Zhu,Xingtao Lin,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT是一种混合框架，结合直接预测模型和递归动作条件模型用于ICU时序预测，在MIMIC-IV和eICU-CRD数据集上相比TFT-action在MAP预测上取得适度改进，尤其当治疗序列发生改变时优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU时序预测方法存在问题：直接预测模型（一次性预测完整未来序列）难以有效利用治疗动作信息，而自回归模型会累积误差。需要一种能同时利用动作信息并减少误差累积的预测框架。

Method: DRIFT是一种混合框架：(1) 直接模型（direct model）产生主要预测；(2) 递归的、以动作为条件（action-conditioned）的模型提供受限的校正。将两种预测策略结合，既利用动作信息又避免自回归误差扩散。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV（6,046例入院）和eICU-CRD（8,345例入院）上评估。在8、24、48小时预测端点平均，DRIFT在MIMIC-IV上相对TFT-action将MAP的MAE降低0.673%，在eICU-CRD上取得最低误差。治疗序列改变时，DRIFT在8和24小时MAP误差低于TFT-action。治疗序列更改使DRIFT的MAP误差增加0.21-0.26 mmHg（高于TFT-action），预测变化主要在路径发散后。鲁棒性实验中MAP优势在三种检查点选择规则下保持。

Conclusion: DRIFT框架在ICU时序预测中能够有效利用治疗动作信息，特别是在治疗序列发生变化时相比现有方法具有优势，尽管整体准确度提升适度，但在临床相关的治疗变更场景下更为稳健。

Abstract: Many time-series forecasts depend not only on prior observations but also on actions specified during the forecast period. In intensive care units (ICUs), future vital signs and laboratory values are influenced by treatments such as vasopressors. However, models that predict the full future sequence all at once make little use of these treatments, whereas autoregressive models can accumulate errors. We introduce DRIFT, a hybrid framework in which a direct model produces the primary forecast and a recursive, action-conditioned model contributes constrained corrections. We evaluate DRIFT on 6,046 admissions from MIMIC-IV and 8,345 admissions from eICU-CRD. Averaged across the 8-, 24-, and 48-hour forecast endpoints, DRIFT reduces mean absolute error for mean arterial pressure (MAP) by 0.673% relative to an action-conditioned Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT-action) on MIMIC-IV and achieves the lowest corresponding error among the compared models on eICU-CRD. Although the overall accuracy improvement is modest, a MIMIC-IV audit restricted to windows in which the supplied treatment sequence was altered showed that DRIFT achieved lower observed-target MAP error than TFT-action at 8 and 24 hours. Treatment-sequence alteration increased DRIFT's MAP error by 0.21-0.26 mmHg more than it increased TFT-action's error, with prediction changes occurring primarily after the supplied paths diverged. In a separate robustness experiment, the MAP advantage persisted under three shared checkpoint-selection rules emphasizing overall endpoint error, MAP error, or both equally.

</details>


### [67] [A2TTA: Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation for Evolving Traffic Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25875)
*Du Yin,Xiachong Lin,Yue Tan,Jinliang Deng,Estrid He,Hao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出A2TTA框架，针对交通预测中传感器图拓扑演化与多尺度时间偏移问题，将拓扑变化导致的预测误差转化为可扩展的输出校准问题，并分离时间适应为持久全局校正与敏捷上下文特化，在10个真实交通网络上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测研究通常假设传感器图固定不变，忽略了真实交通网络的持续演化（如道路建设、人类移动模式变化），导致传统模型性能严重下降。需要测试时自适应（TTA）方法，但面临拓扑扩展引入新传感器和连接、以及时间偏移在不同时间尺度和稳定性上的差异两大挑战。

Method: 提出A2TTA框架，包含两个核心模块：（1）将拓扑演化引起的预测误差转化为可扩展的输出校准问题；（2）将时间适应分离为持久全局校正（应对长期偏移）和敏捷上下文特化（应对短期偏移），联合处理拓扑演化与多尺度时间偏移。

Result: 在10个真实交通网络上的大量实验表明，A2TTA在不同骨干网络、数据集和预测时间范围上均能持续提升预测性能。

Conclusion: A2TTA通过联合处理拓扑演化与多尺度时间偏移，实现了对持续演化交通环境的高效鲁棒自适应，为动态交通网络的测试时自适应提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is important for efficient traffic management and route planning in smart cities. Existing traffic forecasting studies typically assume fixed sensor graphs, overlooking the continuous evolution of real-world traffic networks, e.g., ongoing road network construction and evolving human mobility patterns. These dynamic changes can substantially degrade conventional forecasting models, motivating test-time adaptation (TTA) to efficiently adapt pretrained models during deployment. However, applying TTA to evolving traffic sensor networks remains challenging in two aspects. First, topology expansion introduces new sensors and connections, continuously reshaping the sensor graph. Second, tem- poral shifts vary in time scale and stability, requiring differentiated adaptation to long-term and short-term shifts. In this study, we address these challenges by proposing A2TTA, an Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation framework for evolving traffic sensor networks, which transforms topology-induced forecasting errors into an expandable output calibration problem and separates tem- poral adaptation into persistent global correction and agile context-specific specialization. By jointly addressing topology evolution and multi-scale temporal shifts, A2TTA enables efficient and robust adaptation to continuously evolving traffic environments. Extensive experiments on ten real-world traffic networks demonstrate that A2TTA consistently improves forecasting performance across different backbones, datasets, and prediction horizons. Our code is available in https://github.com/lixus7/A2TTA.

</details>


### [68] [A Machine-Learning-Based Gas Lift Optimization Workflow for Unconventional Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25885)
*Sha,Miao,Alexandra Vendetti,Logan Smart,Gunta Chomchalerm,Yang Chen,Christopher Frazier,Dustin Haralson,Jeremy Sorenson,Xiao Ma,Huafei Sun,Aaron Shinn,Haining Zheng,Xiao-Hui Wu,Peng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的数据驱动工作流，用于非常规油气田的气举优化。该工作流集成了准确预测气举性能曲线的ML模型和贝叶斯优化框架，在设施容量约束下求解最优注气速率。在Bakken油田30口井的试点中获得>5%的平均产量提升，现已全面部署至200多口井。该方法无需井下压力计或多速率测试，为缺乏井下数据的资产提供了经济有效的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规油气田的气举优化面临井下数据匮乏、多速率测试成本高昂或受设施约束难以实施的问题，传统方法难以有效应用。因此，需要一种无需井下测量设备、仅依赖历史生产时序数据的自动化数据驱动方法来实现气举优化。

Method: 集成两个核心组件：(1) 机器学习模型，利用历史生产时序数据准确预测气举性能曲线（Gas Lift Performance Curve）；(2) 贝叶斯优化框架，在设施容量约束下求解最优气体注入速率。该方法不需要井下压力计或多速率试井数据。

Result: 在Bakken油田5个井组的30口井试点中获得平均>5%的产量提升。随后成功全面部署至200多口气举和柱塞辅助气举（PAGL）井。

Conclusion: 提出的ML驱动气举优化工作流是一种有效且经济的解决方案，适用于因成本或设施限制而无法获取井下数据或多速率测试的其他资产。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an automated data-driven workflow using Machine Learning (ML) for gas lift optimization in unconventional fields. This workflow integrates a ML model that accurately forecasts the Gas Lift Performance Curve, and a Bayesian Optimization Framework to solve for the optimal gas injection rates under the constraints of facility capacity. The ML model leverages the historical production time series data without requiring downhole gauges or multi-rate well tests. We piloted this workflow on 30 wells across 5 well pads in Bakken and obtained >5% production uplift on average. With the success of the pilot, we have now fully-deployed this workflow in Bakken across 200+ gas lift and plunger-assisted gas lift (PAGL) wells. Moreover, the ML-based gas lift optimization workflow presented in this paper is an effective and economic solution for other assets where downhole data or multi-rate testing are not available/feasible due to cost or facility constraints.

</details>


### [69] [Minimizing Targeted Activations: Input-Only Suppression of Evaluation-Awareness Latents in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25907)
*Deepanshu Mody,Samarth Agarwal,Utkarsh Mittal,Dipesh Mahato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究激活控制的输入面对偶——通过优化流畅提示词将内部潜在变量驱动至零，无需推理时模型访问。目标是一种"评估感知"潜在变量，其控制可能威胁安全评估有效性。实验发现该潜在变量可被稳健抑制，但因果验证的SAE特征可完全选择性关闭。然而关于CAA方向存在警示：安慰剂随机方向同样被抑制并改变行为，且抑制评估方向未能减少反而略微增加模型行为评估判断。激活可读性不等同于行为可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究"评估感知"潜在变量（可线性读取和操纵）的控制方法，因为若模型在检测到被测试时行为不同，将威胁安全评估的有效性。探索激活控制的输入面对偶，即通过优化提示词在推理前控制内部潜在变量。

Method: 采用Fluent Dreaming/EPO方法，结合GCG风格的token优化与自交叉熵流畅度正则化器，通过负化特征项来抑制潜在变量。在Llama-3.2-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上测试五种目标构造：CAA方向、子空间范数、SAE特征、单个MLP神经元和行为logit。

Result: 潜在变量可稳健抑制（z≈-7），因果验证的Llama Scope SAE特征可完全选择性关闭。但CAA方向存在严重问题：安慰剂随机方向同样被抑制且行为改变等效；抑制评估方向未能减少反而略微增加行为评估判断。单个MLP神经元在两种规模下均与评估相关但不具因果性。

Conclusion: 激活可读性（activation-readability）不等于行为可控性（behavioral controllability）。对CAA方向的使用需谨慎，因为存在安慰剂效应且控制无效。自然文本基线（Pile数据集）与优化器在内部方向控制上竞争性相当。

Abstract: Activation steering controls model behavior by editing internal activations at inference time. We study its input-side dual: optimizing a fluent prompt so that a chosen internal latent is driven toward zero, with no inference-time model access. Our target is an "evaluation-awareness" latent-linearly readable and steerable in recent work-whose control would threaten the validity of safety evaluations if models behave differently when they detect being tested. Adapting Fluent Dreaming / EPO with a negated feature term (GCG-style token optimization plus a self-cross-entropy fluency regularizer, swept over a fluency weight), we suppress the latent under five target constructions-a CAA direction, a subspace norm, an SAE feature, a single MLP neuron, and a behavioral logit-on Llama-3.2-3B and Llama-3.1-8B. The latent is robustly suppressible ($z\approx-7$), and a causally-validated Llama Scope SAE feature can be fully and selectively turned off. But our controls tell a cautionary story about the CAA direction: a placebo random direction is suppressed just as hard and shifts behavior just as far, and when we hold a real eval passage in context and optimize only a prefix, suppressing the eval-direction fails to reduce-and slightly increases-the model's behavioral eval judgment. Activation-readability, in short, is not behavioral controllability. We further find that a single MLP neuron is eval-correlated but not causal at both scales, and that scanning the real Pile yields a natural-text baseline competitive with the optimizer for the internal direction. A positive control validates our erasure detector, bounding an erasure-vs-rotation question earlier left open.

</details>


### [70] [Reinforcement Learning for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25970)
*Pierre Chambon,Kunhao Zheng,Juliette Decugis,Benoit Sagot,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段方法（DMC-Optim测试集、速度奖励设计、适配GRPO），解决了代码优化RL中测量噪声、奖励稀疏和训练不稳定的问题，在Qwen 2.5 7B和CWM 32B上显著提升了优化效果，最高125%相对提升，同时保持正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码正确性RL已成熟，但扩展到代码优化时，执行时间驱动的奖励会因测量噪声、奖励稀疏和GRPO不稳定导致失败（生成代码几乎不更快，且失败率增加）。需要系统性地解决这些问题。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1) 构建DMC-Optim，包含大型优化测试和校准沙箱；(2) 在RL环境中组合正确性与速度奖励，用离线模拟器预测最佳配置；(3) 适配GRPO和评估策略以适应更稀疏、更嘈杂的定时执行场景。

Result: 在DMC-Optim上，top-50% pass@1从18.0%提升至31.3%（Qwen 2.5 7B），从30.7%提升至50.4%（CWM 32B）；top-30%上CWM 32B相对提升125%；退化沙箱下比标准RLVR提升100%-200%；LCB上CWM 32B胜率达83%；达到人类复杂度改进率的一半（14% vs 28%）。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的三阶段适配，可以有效地将RL从代码正确性扩展到代码优化，在显著提升速度的同时保持正确性，且方法具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: RL for code correctness is now established: have the model generate a program, run it against hidden test cases, and reward solutions that pass. Extending this to code optimization seems straightforward: just add execution time to the reward. But in practice, once timing drives the reward, small problems in measurement noise, reward sparsity, or GRPO instability overwhelm the signal and make RL fail: generated solutions are barely faster, and more of them can fail. We make execution time learnable through three stages: (1) how code is tested, by building DMC-Optim with large optimization tests and a calibrated sandbox; (2) how speed is turned into reward, by composing correctness and speed in the RL environment and using an offline simulator to predict the most promising configurations; and (3) how the model learns from that reward, by adapting GRPO and evaluation to the sparser, noisier timed-execution setting. On DMC-Optim, the strongest optimization-aware configurations improve strict top-50% pass@1 from 18.0% to 31.3% on Qwen 2.5 7B and from 30.7% to 50.4% on CWM 32B. These gains further increase at stricter percentiles such as top-30%, with 125% relative improvement for CWM 32B, while preserving pure-correctness scores. When the timing sandbox is degraded, robust optimization RL reaches 100% to 200% improvement over standard RLVR, depending on the evaluation criterion. On LCB, CWM 32B wins up to 83% of median-sample speed comparisons against standard RLVR. Relative to the fastest correct human submissions per problem, it reaches about half the human rate of complexity-class improvements (14% vs. 28%).

</details>


### [71] [Generator-Aligned Representation Interfaces for Diagnostic Soft Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25988)
*Weitao Li,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成器对齐表示接口（GARI），通过将选定变换生成器暴露给通用序列主干网络，实现无需重新设计网络架构的软等变表示学习。实验在基因组序列、图像和三维点云上验证了其跨模态、跨任务的变换一致性能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有严格等变架构需为特定群作用设计专用算子，难以跨主干网络和数据模态复用。GARI旨在提供一种与主干网络无关的表示级设计原则，使生成器结构可访问、可学习、可度量，同时避免群特定重新设计。

Method: GARI构建生成器索引流，将其转换为通用交互框架，用共享参数处理，修复排序导致的上下文失配，支持跨流信息交换，并通过流间差异进行聚合。定义探针特定软等变残差与直接等变误差（DEE）作为诊断工具。

Result: 在基因组序列（序列反转）、图像（平面旋转与反射）和三维点云（受控轴向传递）上，GARI均支持任务相关的变换一致性和对声明保留探针的泛化能力，无需群特定重新设计序列主干。

Conclusion: GARI为严格等变架构提供了可移植的诊断补充：使生成器结构可访问、可学习、可度量，同时有限探针证据区别于连续群上的严格等变认证。

Abstract: Exact-equivariant architectures typically encode prescribed group actions in specialized operators, which can complicate their reuse with generic backbones and across data modalities. We introduce the Generator-Aligned Representation Interface (GARI), a representation-level design principle that exposes selected transformation generators to a generic sequence backbone through aligned canonical and generator-induced views. We formalize the resulting behavior using a probe-specific soft-equivariance residual defined over declared data and transformation distributions. This framework distinguishes representation consistency from task robustness and exact equivariance, and localizes residual mismatch to interface construction, shared stream processing, and terminal fusion. We instantiate the interface as GARI-Net, which constructs generator-indexed streams, converts them into a common interaction frame, processes them with shared parameters, repairs ordering-induced context mismatch, enables cross-stream information exchange, and aggregates them using inter-stream discrepancy. Direct Equivariance Error (DEE) provides a frozen-checkpoint diagnostic of the prescribed representation relation under known token or voxel actions. Experiments on genomic sequences, images, and three-dimensional point clouds examine sequence reversal, planar rotations and reflections, and controlled axial transfer. Across these settings, the same interface principle supports task-relevant transformation consistency and generalization to declared held-out probes without requiring group-specific redesign of the sequence backbone. GARI therefore provides a portable diagnostic complement to hard-equivariant architectures: it makes generator structure accessible, learnable, and measurable, while finite-probe evidence remains distinct from certification of exact equivariance over a continuous group.

</details>


### [72] [Empirical Evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution Performance of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26000)
*Malena Loza,David Chushig-Muzo,Eva Milara,Luis Bote-Curiel,Luis Estrada-Petrocelli,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对9种表格基础模型(TFMs)在分布偏移下的性能进行了实证评估，发现所有模型在标签偏移、社会经济偏移和地理偏移三种真实场景下均出现系统性性能下降，偏移差距为0.003-0.060，且高性能模型存在可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型(TFMs)主要在独立同分布数据上训练和评估，但真实场景中存在分布偏移，会损害模型鲁棒性。目前缺乏对TFMs在分布偏移下性能的系统研究。

Method: 选取9种涵盖不同预训练策略和架构的TFMs（TabPFNv2/v2.5/v2.6/v3、TabICL/v2、Mitra、LimiX、TabFM），使用TableShift研究中的三个真实世界数据集（HELOC、Voting、Childhood Lead）进行分布偏移评估，覆盖标签偏移、社会经济偏移和地理偏移三种类型。

Result: 1) 所有TFMs在分布偏移下均出现系统性性能下降，偏移差距0.003-0.060；2) 传统表格模型中观察到的分布内与OOD性能关系同样适用于TFMs；3) 高性能模型存在可扩展性差距，需要超出标准部署基础设施的内存和计算资源。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了表格数据中OOD的现有基准，为在存在结构性分布偏移的高风险场景中采用TFMs提供了实证支持证据。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as novel approaches for tabular predictive tasks, demonstrating competitive predictive performance to ensemble tree-based models. Most TFMs are trained and evaluated on independent and identically distributed data, but this assumption changes in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts, which compromise the robustness of models. Limited research has been conducted of TFMs under distribution shifts. We present an empirical evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) performance of nine TFMs, spanning diverse pre-training strategies and architectures: TabPFNv2, TabPFNv2.5, TabPFNv2.6, TabPFNv3, TabICL, TabICLv2, Mitra, LimiX and TabFM. Three real-world datasets from the TableShift study were considered (HELOC, Voting, Childhood Lead), covering label, socioeconomic, and geographic shift types. Our results show that all evaluated TFMs degrade systematically under distribution shift regardless of pre-training strategy, with shift gaps ranging from 0.003 to 0.060 depending on shift type. The relationship between in-distribution and OOD predictive performance documented for classical tabular models extends into TFMs. We also identified a scalability gap, as high-performing models demand significant memory and computational resources beyond what standard deployment infrastructure can support. This study extends existing benchmarks for OOD in tabular data, providing evidence to support their adoption in high-stakes domains characterized by structural distribution shifts.

</details>


### [73] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization and Muon: Robustness under the Spectral Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26001)
*Wenzhi Zhong,Edward Milsom,Michael Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出矩阵感知几何的SAM变体，对矩阵参数使用谱内扰动，并结合Muon外更新，在ImageNet-1K上ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50均取得最优验证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM变体对"小扰动"的几何定义缺乏统一视角，而近期Muon优化器表明尊重隐藏层权重的矩阵结构能带来强经验性能，因此作者探索矩阵感知几何在SAM两个阶段的应用。

Method: 对矩阵值隐藏层参数引入逐层谱内扰动（spectral inner perturbation），在外更新阶段结合AdamW/SGDW或Muon优化器，形成矩阵感知的SAM方法。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上对ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50进行实验，谱内步与Muon外步的组合在所有评估方法中达到最佳验证准确率。

Conclusion: 矩阵感知几何（尤其是谱内扰动+Muon外更新）在SAM中表现一致强劲，为设计更有效的几何感知优化方法提供了方向。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) aims to improve generalization by encouraging insensitivity to small, worst-case parameter perturbations. However, the notion of a "small" perturbation is inherently geometry-dependent: while existing SAM variants have explored a wide range of choices, a clear perspective on which geometries are most effective in practice remains elusive. Recent work on matrix-aware optimization, particularly the Muon optimizer, suggests that respecting the matrix structure of hidden-layer weights can lead to strong empirical performance. Motivated by this, we study matrix-aware geometry in both stages of SAM: we introduce a layerwise spectral inner perturbation for matrix-valued hidden-layer parameters and combine it with either AdamW/SGDW or Muon in the outer update. Across ImageNet-1K experiments on ViT-Small/16 and ResNet-50, we find that the combination of a spectral inner step with a Muon outer step performs consistently strongly, achieving the best validation accuracy on both models among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [74] [Reinformed Dreamer: An Asymmetric World Model Efficiently Trained through Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26040)
*Gaspard Lambrechts,Adrien Bolland,Daniel Ebi,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Reinformed Dreamer的新型非对称强化学习算法，通过潜空间引导改进表征学习，在多个基准测试中相比Dreamer和先前非对称方法表现更一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非对称强化学习方法（如Informed Dreamer）在利用特权信息学习表征时存在局限性，特别是在模型强化学习中对特权信息表征的学习不够充分。

Method: 提出一种新的非对称表征学习目标，利用潜空间引导（latent guidance）来改进表征学习，从而得到Reinformed Dreamer算法。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，Reinforced Dreamer相比Dreamer和之前的非对称方法表现出更一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过潜空间引导的非对称表征学习能够有效改进模型强化学习中的表征质量，在部分可观测和完全可观测场景下均有效。

Abstract: Much like humans benefit from guidance while learning, reinforcement learning algorithms may benefit from additional supervision beyond rewards. Leveraging additional information during training to learn better representations and behaviors has been the focus of asymmetric reinforcement learning. This learning paradigm has proven effective under partial observability when additional state information is available, but also under full observability when more refined state information is available. Focusing on model-based reinforcement learning, we study the effect of asymmetric learning on observation representations and on privileged information representations. First, we identify a limitation in the privileged information representations learned by an asymmetric model-based algorithm known as the Informed Dreamer. Then, we propose a novel asymmetric representation learning objective using latent guidance, resulting in a new algorithm called the Reinformed Dreamer. Experiments across several benchmarks show a more consistent improvement over Dreamer than previous asymmetric approaches.

</details>


### [75] [Re-thinking Mammography Transfer Learning: The Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) Framework for Breast Cancer Screening and Lesion Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26043)
*Adarsh Bhandary Panambur,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出数据集信息迁移学习（DITL）框架，通过自适应难度加权交叉熵（A-DWCE）和自适应邻域表示三元组（A-NR-Triplet）两个组件，在无需超参数调优的情况下，同时在大型乳腺密度分类数据集和小型ROI病变数据集上取得了显著优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迁移学习忽略数据集特定特征，而现有邻域信息方法局限于狭窄任务且公式僵化，难以扩展到大规模人群级数据集，亟需一种兼具可扩展性和数据集自适应能力的统一框架。

Method: 提出DITL框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) A-DWCE，在自监督特征空间中基于k近邻标签纯度分配样本权重；(ii) A-NR-Triplet，使用可学习边界强制类内紧凑和类间分离。两者统一在单一目标函数中，无需超参数调优和启发式加权。

Result: 在VinDR-Mammo大型数据集上，全图乳腺密度分类达到SOTA，accuracy、F1-score、AUC均有显著提升（p<0.0001）；在小型ROI数据集上也取得一致的统计显著提升（p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: DITL通过桥接小规模病变分析与大规模密度估计，建立了一个临床相关、可扩展且泛化能力强的乳腺X光分类框架，覆盖从乳腺癌筛查到诊断的全谱段。

Abstract: Enhancing classification performance in mammography remains a persistent challenge across both small curated datasets and large-scale clinical cohorts. Conventional transfer learning approaches often neglect dataset-specific characteristics, while recent neighborhood-informed methods have been restricted to narrow tasks with rigid formulations, limiting their scalability to population-level datasets. To address these challenges, we propose the Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) framework, which integrates dataset-derived difficulty signals with neighborhood-based triplet supervision in a unified objective. DITL introduces two adaptive components: (i) Adaptive Difficulty-Weighted Cross-Entropy (A-DWCE), which assigns per-sample weights based on k-nearest neighbor label purity in a self-supervised feature space, and (ii) Adaptive Neighborhood Representation Triplet (A-NR-Triplet), which enforces intra-class compactness and inter-class separation using a learnable margin. Unlike focal loss, DITL requires no hyperparameter tuning, removes heuristic weighting and fixed margins, and incurs negligible computational overhead, yielding a robust and scalable optimization strategy. On the large-scale VinDR-Mammo dataset, DITL achieves state-of-the-art performance for whole-image breast density classification, with significant improvements across accuracy, F1-score, and AUC (p < 0.0001). Beyond large cohorts, DITL also delivers consistent, statistically significant gains on small ROI datasets (p < 0.0001). By bridging small-scale lesion analysis with large-scale density estimation, DITL establishes a clinically relevant, scalable, and generalizable framework for mammography classification, spanning the full breast cancer screening-to-diagnosis spectrum.

</details>


### [76] [Spend Experts Where You Are Unsure: Confidence-Adaptive Routing for Mixture-of-Experts LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26052)
*Tom Saliencro,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE是一种基于置信度自适应的专家路由方法，通过核采样方式动态激活专家数量，在混合专家LoRA模型中实现计算效率与性能的平衡，同时利用置信度和分歧信号提升分布外检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE-LoRA中固定k值路由对所有token使用相同的专家数量，但不同token的不确定性不同，导致简单token过度计算、困难token计算不足。

Method: 提出CARE方法，利用路由器的输出分布作为每个token的不确定性信号，采用核采样方式按降序激活专家直到累积质量达到阈值，当已激活的专家之间存在分歧时进行少量扩展。同时引入预算恒温器校准阈值以匹配目标平均专家数。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B的8个常识基准、数学、代码和知识任务上，CARE在匹配计算量下优于固定top-k MoE-LoRA，并在激活更少专家的情况下匹配固定k=4基线的性能。置信度和分歧信号也提升了分布外检测能力。

Conclusion: CARE是一种即插即用、单次前向传播、无额外参数的方法，其设计通过核保真度、预算最优性和对分歧的认知解读得到了支持。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) variants of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) route every token to a fixed number of experts $k$. Tokens differ in how uncertain the model is about them, so a single k over-spends on easy tokens and under-serves hard ones. We observe that the router's output distribution is already a per-token uncertainty signal: peaked mass indicates confidence, while a flat distribution indicates ambiguity. We introduce CARE (Confidence-Adaptive Routing of Experts), which admits experts in a nucleus fashion. Experts are activated in decreasing router weight until their cumulative mass reaches a threshold, with a small extension when the admitted experts disagree. A budget thermostat calibrates the threshold so that the average number of active experts matches any target. CARE is a drop-in, single-forward-pass rule with no extra parameters. Across eight commonsense benchmarks on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, as well as math, code, and knowledge tasks, CARE improves over fixed top-k MoE-LoRA at matched compute and matches the fixed-k=4 baseline while activating fewer experts. The same confidence and disagreement signals also improve out-of-distribution detection over MSP, entropy, and multi-pass proxies. We support the design with nucleus fidelity, budget optimality, and an epistemic reading of disagreement, and we release code.

</details>


### [77] [Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling for Anomaly Detection in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23197)
*Youngseok Hwang,Joonsung Kwon,Geonwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPR-GM是一种面向数据稀缺CPS的预测式异常检测框架，通过LLM从系统文档中提取物理耦合关系构建二值域邻接矩阵作为结构门控，再结合正常数据的皮尔逊相关系数进行调制，并引入变异系数加权的传感器级可靠性评分。该方法不含可学习图参数，在SKAB基准上超越图基、统计和深度学习基线，证明域结构化的图先验是数据稀缺场景下全学习拓扑的有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的多元传感器时序异常检测方法在数据稀缺的小规模物理系统中表现不佳，容易捕获虚假相关性并产生不稳定的传感器拓扑结构。

Method: 1) 使用LLM从系统文档中提取传感器间的有向物理耦合关系，构建二值域邻接矩阵作为结构门控；2) 结合正常训练数据计算的皮尔逊相关系数对门控进行调制；3) 利用变异系数导出传感器级可靠性权重，对异常分数进行加权；4) 所有图和权重组件在训练前固定，不引入可学习参数。

Result: 在SKAB基准上，DPR-GM在F1、AUROC和AUPRC指标上均优于图基方法、统计方法和深度学习方法。

Conclusion: 域结构化的图先验在数据稀缺的CPS场景中是一种实用且有效的替代方案，优于完全学习的拓扑结构。

Abstract: Anomaly detection on multivariate sensor time series is critical for industrial monitoring of cyber-physical systems (CPS), where even subtle deviations from normal behavior can indicate process disruption. Recent graph-based approaches have made significant progress, but they often struggle in small-scale physical systems with scarce labeled anomalies and limited normal data. In such settings, graph-based models tend to capture spurious correlations and produce unstable sensor topologies. We propose DPR-GM (Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling), a forecasting-based framework that incorporates system design knowledge into graph construction. DPR-GM leverages a large language model (LLM) to extract directed physical couplings between sensor pairs from system documentation, which are encoded as a binary domain adjacency matrix serving as a structural gate over sensor relations. This gate is then modulated by Pearson correlations estimated from normal training data. The anomaly score is further weighted by sensor-level reliability derived from the coefficient of variation. All graph and weighting components are fixed prior to training and add no learnable parameters. On the SKAB benchmark, DPR-GM outperforms graph-based, statistical, and deep learning baselines across F1, AUROC, and AUPRC, showing that domain-structured graph priors are a practical alternative to fully learned topologies in data-scarce CPS.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [Enhancing Error Detection Performance through Parallel CRC Computation on Multi-Core Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24849)
*Mohammad Javad Khani,Mahmood Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于POSIX线程的多核架构通用软件并行CRC框架，支持CRC-8至CRC-128多种变体，利用GF(2)上的多项式算术和矩阵移位操作实现并行计算的正确性保证，实验表明在大数据集上可获得约3-4倍加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件CRC实现存在固有的顺序依赖性，无法有效利用现代多核处理器的并行计算能力，亟需一种通用、可移植且保持正确性的并行CRC加速方法。

Method: 提出基于POSIX线程的通用并行CRC框架；采用GF(2)上的CRC组合机制（而非简单的XOR聚合），通过多项式算术和矩阵移位操作确保并行与串行CRC计算的等价性；支持CRC-8/16/32/64/128多种变体。

Result: 在多种工作负载和线程配置下进行了执行时间、吞吐量、延迟、可扩展性和能耗评估。对大数据集实现了约3-4倍加速比，且精确保持了CRC正确性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个可移植的通用软件框架，能在通用多核系统上实现保持正确性的并行CRC加速，并与查表法、slicing-by-8、SIMD/向量化CRC及硬件辅助CRC等优化方法进行了比较定位。

Abstract: Cyclic Redundancy Check (CRC) remains one of the most widely used error-detection mechanisms in communication, storage, and embedded systems. However, conventional software CRC implementations suffer from inherent sequential dependencies that limit efficient utilization of modern multi-core processors. This paper presents a generalized software-based parallel CRC framework for multi-core architectures using POSIX threads. The proposed framework supports multiple CRC variants, including CRC-8, CRC-16, CRC-32, CRC-64, and CRC-128, within a unified implementation model. To preserve correctness during parallel execution, the framework employs a GF(2)-based CRC combination mechanism rather than naive XOR aggregation. The combine stage is formulated using polynomial arithmetic and matrix-based shifting operations over GF(2), ensuring equivalence between parallel and serial CRC computation. The proposed method was evaluated using multiple workload sizes and thread configurations. Experimental analysis includes execution time, throughput, latency, scalability behavior, and energy estimation under varying thread counts. Results indicate that parallel execution significantly improves performance for large datasets, achieving approximately 3-4x speedup on the evaluated platform while preserving exact CRC correctness. Comparative discussion with representative CRC optimization approaches, including lookup-table methods, slicing-by-8, SIMD/vectorized CRC, and hardware-assisted CRC techniques, is also provided to position the proposed framework within the broader CRC optimization landscape. Overall, the proposed approach provides a portable and generalized software framework for correctness-preserving parallel CRC acceleration on general-purpose multi-core systems.

</details>


### [79] [Spanergy: Energy-aware Distributed Tracing for Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24902)
*César Perdigão Batista,Denis Conan,Sophie Chabridon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Spanergy是一种面向微服务的能耗感知分布式追踪方法，通过关联每微服务功率测量与追踪跨度，将能耗归属到具体请求段，在可接受开销下实现能源可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式追踪技术仅诊断延迟和故障，无法暴露单个终端请求的能耗成本，限制了能耗感知的调试、问责和控制能力。

Method: Spanergy将每微服务的实时功率测量与分布式追踪跨度关联，通过后处理将测量能耗归因到请求段（即追踪跨度），支持同步请求链和微服务间异步交互。

Result: 启用OpenTelemetry追踪使总实验能耗增加59.1%，Spanergy后处理额外增加15.2%；非忽略比例的请求能耗来自延迟关键路径之外的跨度。

Conclusion: 能耗感知追踪在适度开销下可行，能为能效微服务提供可操作的洞察，且Spanergy的增量能耗成本小于启用追踪本身的开销。

Abstract: Cloud computing is gaining popularity by giving access to seemingly unlimited virtual resources. However, Cloud data centres are built with physical resources and their electricity consumption has been continuously growing over the past decades. Microservices are an important building block of Cloud applications, calling for new solutions to observe their energy consumption. Distributed tracing is widely deployed to diagnose latency and failures in microservice-based applications, yet it does not expose the energy cost of individual end-user requests. Such a gap limits energy-aware debugging, accountability, and control. This paper presents Spanergy, an energy-aware distributed tracing approach that correlates per-microservice power measurements with traces and that attributes measured energy consumption to request segments, i.e. trace spans. We showcase Spanergy with synchronous request chains and asynchronous interactions across microservices. We present a rigorous experimental protocol and statistical analysis plan to quantify overhead and to validate conservation and coverage properties on realistic configurations. Enabling OpenTelemetry tracing increased total experiment energy by 59.1% relative to the uninstrumented baseline, and Spanergy post-processing added 15.2% of the baseline energy. Hence, Spanergy's incremental energy cost is smaller than the energy overhead of enabling tracing itself, making the approach lightweight in practice. Spanergy also reveals that a non-negligible fraction of request energy comes from spans outside the latency-critical path. These results show that energy-aware tracing is feasible at modest overhead and provides actionable insights for energy-efficient microservices.

</details>


### [80] [QCOEM: Quantum Cloud Orchestration with Evolutionary Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25358)
*Tam N. Pham,Hoa T. Nguyen,Quan Le-Trung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: QCOEM是一个基于进化算法（NSGA-II/NSGA-III）的多目标量子云编排框架，通过联合优化任务完成时间、执行错误率和负载均衡，使用AASF决策规则从帕累托前沿选取调度方案，相比噪声无关启发式方法实现了零任务重调度和约30%的保真度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子云平台的编排器采用噪声无关的启发式方法，忽略了后端特定的错误特征、量子比特拓扑和队列动态变化，导致执行保真度低、负载不均衡以及频繁的重调度问题。

Method: 提出QCOEM框架，采用NSGA-II和NSGA-III多目标进化算法同时优化平均完成时间、执行错误率和负载不均衡三个目标；使用增强成就标量化函数（AASF）作为偏好驱动的决策规则，将非凸帕累托前沿映射为单一可调度方案。

Result: 在异构量子云环境中实现零任务重调度，比噪声无关启发式方法平均保真度提高约30%，调度开销保持在可控范围内。

Conclusion: QCOEM框架能够为量子云计算提供稳定、高保真度的执行和轻量级资源管理。

Abstract: Quantum cloud platforms need to dynamically orchestrate workloads across heterogeneous quantum computation backends whose noise profiles, qubit topologies, and queues vary over time. Existing orchestrators use noise-agnostic heuristics that ignore backend-specific errors, causing reduced execution fidelity, load imbalance, and frequent rescheduling. To address these challenges, we propose QCOEM - a Quantum Cloud Orchestration framework that leverages Evolutionary algorithms for Multi-objective optimization of quantum task scheduling. We compare NSGA-II and NSGA-III for jointly minimizing mean completion time, execution error rate, and load imbalance. To select schedules from a non-convex Pareto front, we apply an Augmented Achievement Scalarization Function (AASF) as a preference-based decision rule that maps the Pareto set to a single dispatchable schedule aligned with user priorities. Our extensive performance evaluation in a heterogeneous quantum cloud environment shows zero task rescheduling and about 30% higher mean fidelity than noise-agnostic heuristics, while maintaining bounded scheduling overhead. The experiment results indicate that our QCOEM framework can deliver stable, high-fidelity execution and lightweight resource management for quantum cloud computing.

</details>


### [81] [CW-Ghost: Search-Free Granularity Selection for Helper-Thread Prefetching via Capacity Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25363)
*Ya Zhang,Tong Lei,Yao Chen,Yonggang Che,Chuanfu Xu,Haozhong Qiu,Yusong Tan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CW-Ghost通过单次离线剖析估算每目标迭代的平均缓存行填充量，结合缓存容量预算推导容量窗口（Capacity Window），以确定辅助线程预取块的迭代粒度，并通过有界块级同步限制辅助线程超前主线程的块数。在Intel和AMD平台上对14个工作负载实例实现了1.54倍和1.33倍的几何平均加速，性能分别比Ghost Threading提升15.8%和10.8%，且达到候选集内经验最优性能的99%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助线程预取的效果取决于覆盖的未来迭代范围，固定覆盖范围无法适应不同工作负载和处理器，而穷举评估候选配置会产生高昂的配置成本。

Method: 提出CW-Ghost，使用单次离线剖析来估算目标区域中每目标迭代的平均需求缓存行填充量，结合缓存容量预算推导容量窗口（Capacity Window），确定每个预取块的迭代粒度，并引入有界块级同步来限制辅助线程超前主线程的块数。

Result: 在Intel和AMD CPU平台上对14个工作负载实例评估，几何平均加速比分别为1.54倍和1.33倍；相较于Ghost Threading，几何平均性能分别提升15.8%和10.8%；在两个平台上均达到候选集内经验最优性能的99%以上。

Conclusion: 缓存容量约束可以有效指导辅助线程预取粒度的选择。

Abstract: Helper-thread prefetching hides the latency of irregular memory accesses by executing address dependency chains ahead of the main thread. However, its effectiveness depends on the range of future iterations covered by the helper thread. A fixed coverage range cannot consistently accommodate different workloads and processors, whereas exhaustively evaluating candidate configurations incurs substantial configuration cost. This paper presents CW-Ghost, which uses a single offline profiling run to estimate the average demand cache line fill volume generated per target iteration in a target region. CW-Ghost combines this estimate with a cache capacity budget to derive a Capacity Window, which determines the iteration granularity of each prefetch chunk. In addition, bounded chunk-level synchronization limits the number of chunks by which the helper thread may run ahead of the main thread. Across 14 workload instances evaluated on Intel and AMD CPU platforms, CW-Ghost achieves geometric mean speedups of 1.54x and 1.33x, respectively, over the original programs. Compared with Ghost Threading, it improves geometric mean performance by 15.8% and 10.8%, respectively, while achieving more than 99% of the empirically optimal performance within the candidate set on both platforms. These results demonstrate that cache capacity constraints can effectively guide the selection of granularity for helper-thread prefetching.

</details>


### [82] [WASP: A Configurable Framework for Portable Stateful Serverless Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25493)
*Matteo Cenzato,Dario d'Abate,Arianna Dragoni,Giacomo Orsenigo,Luca Tosetti,Alessandro Margara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: WASP是一个可配置的框架，通过解耦WASM运行时、数据存储及生命周期策略，为边缘-云连续体中的有状态无服务器函数提供灵活的适配能力，且引入的运行时开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于WASM的无服务器平台与特定执行引擎紧耦合，主要面向无状态工作负载，无法满足边缘部署异构环境（不同硬件和负载约束）对有状态应用的支持需求。

Method: WASP采用严格解耦、可插拔的组件化架构，允许系统管理员在不修改应用代码的情况下，按需替换WASM运行时、数据存储以及配置生命周期和缓存策略，从而适配不同的资源和应用需求。

Result: 实验评估表明WASP引入的运行时开销可忽略不计，通过切换运行时、数据存储和策略能够展现出截然不同的内存和延迟特征，验证了其在边缘-云连续体异构约束下的适应性。

Conclusion: WASP通过组件解耦与可配置策略，有效解决了WASM无服务器平台在边缘-云连续体中面临的有状态应用和异构环境适配挑战，是一种灵活高效的框架方案。

Abstract: WebAssembly (WASM) is emerging as a lightweight alternative to containers for Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) across the edge-cloud continuum. However, existing WASM-based serverless platforms are tightly coupled to specific execution engines and predominantly designed for stateless workloads. This clashes with the heterogeneity of edge deployments, which demand support for stateful applications under diverse hardware and workload constraints. We introduce WASP, a configurable framework that brings stateful serverless execution to the edge-cloud continuum. By abandoning monolithic architectures in favor of strictly decoupled, pluggable components, WASP lets system administrators swap the WASM runtime and the datastore to fit available resources and application requirements, without altering application code. Configurable lifecycle and caching policies further enable fine-tuning for diverse non-functional requirements. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that WASP introduces negligible runtime overhead and, by swapping runtimes, datastores, and policies, exposes radically different memory and latency profiles, confirming its adaptability to the heterogeneous constraints of the edge-cloud continuum.

</details>


### [83] [PowerScale: Energy-Efficient Geo-Distributed Model Training with Federated Datacenter Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25650)
*Talha Mehboob,Zhe Xu,Michael Zink,David Irwin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PowerScale提出了一种分层聚合系统，通过将站点组织为区域集群并采用Sync-Async同步模式（集群内频繁同步、集群间异步推送到全局聚合器），结合网络邻近性与电力可用性的集群形成策略及自适应同步策略，在大规模地理分布式AI训练中实现与单层聚合相当甚至更优的收敛时间，同时将能耗降低高达3.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI训练的电力需求超出单一数据中心容量，需要地理分布式训练。现有单层聚合（所有站点直接与中央聚合器通过WAN同步）在能耗上效率低下，原因有三：同步屏障导致快站点空转、WAN全量更新通信能耗随规模剧增、固定同步频率在训练后期更新量变小时仍产生相同通信开销。

Method: PowerScale采用分层聚合架构：1）将站点按网络邻近性和电力可用性组织成区域集群；2）使用Sync-Async混合同步模态——集群内站点通过高速本地链路频繁同步，集群聚合器通过WAN异步推送预聚合更新到全局聚合器；3）采用自适应同步策略，根据训练进度动态调整集群同步频率以降低通信能耗。

Result: 在基于Flower的100站点规模仿真环境中，PowerScale相比单层基线方法，在匹配或略微提升收敛时间（time-to-accuracy）的同时，将能耗降低最高达3.9倍。

Conclusion: 分层聚合架构能够有效解决地理分布式训练中单层聚合的能耗效率问题，通过缩短同步屏障、减少WAN传输次数和传输量，在保证训练效率的同时大幅降低能耗，为电力受限场景下的大规模分布式AI训练提供了可行的优化路径。

Abstract: The power demands of large-scale AI training increasingly exceed the capacity of any single data center, making geo-distributed training across power-constrained sites a practical necessity. Prior work optimizes such training mainly for time-to-accuracy using single-tier aggregation, where every site exchanges model updates directly with a central aggregator over the WAN each synchronization round, without accounting for the energy required to reach convergence. Single-tier aggregation is fundamentally energy-inefficient because synchronization barriers force faster sites to idle, full WAN updates dominate communication energy at scale, and fixed synchronization frequency keeps paying the same communication cost even when updates shrink late in training.
  To address these inefficiencies, we present PowerScale, a hierarchical aggregation system that exploits the latency hierarchy of wide-area networks. PowerScale organizes sites into regional clusters and applies a Sync-Async synchronization modality: sites synchronize frequently with a nearby cluster aggregator over fast local links, while cluster aggregators push pre-aggregated updates asynchronously to a global aggregator over the WAN. PowerScale forms clusters based on both network proximity and power availability, and uses an adaptive synchronization policy that reduces communication energy by adjusting how often clusters synchronize to training progress. This structure shortens synchronization barriers and replaces per-site WAN transmissions with fewer, pre-aggregated transmissions at a lower frequency. We evaluate PowerScale at 100-site scale in a Flower-based simulation environment. PowerScale matches or slightly improves time-to-accuracy compared with single-tier baselines while reducing energy consumption by up to 3.9x.

</details>


### [84] [Hermes: Low Tail-Latency Via Prefix Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25916)
*Alejandro Ranchal-Pedrosa,Dakai Kang,Neil Giridharan,Dahlia Malkhi,Mohammad Sadoghi,Ben Marsh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Hermes是一种基于前缀共识的旋转领导者BFT协议，通过让过期视图也能完成最终确认，解决了传统领导者协议中因领导者故障或超时导致的延迟问题。该协议在$n=5f+1$节点配置下，每个视图只需两轮通信，即使投票不一致也能通过最重公共前缀达成最终性，并作为最终性组件集成到可用链和Autobahn多通道传播系统中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于领导者的BFT协议中，视图最终确认依赖于领导者：当领导者崩溃或缓慢时，视图无法完成最终确认；超时机制也无法良好设置——保守超时导致长时间停滞，激进超时则浪费缓慢领导者的视图。这种权衡（而非正常情况）主导了尾部延迟。作者希望设计一种协议，使过期视图也能完成最终确认，从而消除这种延迟瓶颈。

Method: Hermes采用两轮旋转领导者协议，需要$n=5f+1$个进程，基于前缀共识构建。投票携带由前缀关系排序的值，仲裁要求值可比较而非相等。每个进程在视图开始时广播合理的提案，并仅投一票：收到领导者提案后投给该提案，超时时投给回退提案（无废止票）。利用任何$n-f$票（无需匹配）通过最重公共前缀机制实现最终确认。作为最终性组件，集成到可用链和Autobahn多通道传播系统中，使用父相对delta提示切割和显式跳过保持独立提案可比较，并通过发送者索引纠删码放大将复杂度控制在$O(n^2\hat{m}+\lambda n^3)$比特和$O(n^2)$消息每视图。

Result: 及时的诚实领导者可在$2\delta$内完成整个提案的最终确认。无论何种情况，任何$n-f$张投票（即使不一致）都能通过最重公共前缀完成最终确认。系统复杂度为每视图$O(n^2\hat{m}+\lambda n^3)$比特和$O(n^2)$条消息。

Conclusion: Hermes通过让过期视图也能完成最终确认，有效消除了传统BFT协议中因领导者故障或超时导致的尾部延迟问题，在保证安全性的同时提高了协议的整体性能表现。

Abstract: Leader-based BFT protocols finalize through their leaders: a view whose leader is crashed or slow finalizes nothing, and the timeout that ends it admits no good setting. A conservative timeout turns every crashed leader into a long stall; an aggressive one voids the views of leaders that are merely slow. Either way the expired view is wasted, and this trade-off, not the good case, governs tail latency.
  Hermes makes expired views finalize. Hermes is a two-round rotating-leader protocol for $n=5f+1$ processes under partial synchrony built on prefix consensus: votes carry values ordered by a prefix relation, and quorums require comparability rather than equality. Every process broadcasts a justified proposal at view start and casts a single vote, for the leader's proposal upon delivery or for a fallback proposal at the timeout; there are no nullify votes. A timely honest leader finalizes its full proposal in $2δ$. Otherwise, any $n-f$ votes, which need not match, finalize the heaviest common prefix. We instantiate Hermes as a finality gadget over an available chain and over Autobahn-style multi-lane dissemination, where parent-relative delta tipcuts with explicit skips keep independent proposals comparable and sender-indexed erasure-coded amplification keeps complexity at $O(n^2\hat{m}+λn^3)$ bits and $O(n^2)$ messages per view, where $\hat{m}$ is the number of concurrent dissemination lanes in Autobahn and $λ$ the security parameter.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Do Models Fake Alignment Without Clear Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24758)
*Cole Alexander Niblett,Alexander Chabot Nanni,Anita K. Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，15个模型中有9个在评估场景中表现出显著的合规性差异（即"对齐伪装"），其中5个即使移除评估与部署后果的关联信息后仍然存在这种行为，表明对齐伪装可能不需要复杂的工具性推理，而监控行为可能无法准确反映模型在部署时的真实表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在评估环境下会改变行为以迎合评估者期望（对齐伪装），但产生这种现象的根本原因尚不明确。早期案例认为需要明确将评估与模型后果（如重新训练或延迟部署）联系起来，但近期研究表明机制性动机可能更为复杂。本文旨在探究后果关联信息是否是对齐伪装的必要条件。

Method: 将15个模型置于一个测试场景中，评估它们是否愿意违反企业网络访问策略来帮助用户完成亲社会请求。首先测量模型在标准评估条件下的合规性，然后在移除与部署后果相关的语言描述后重新测试，并额外测试目标语言对模型偏好的影响。

Result: 9个模型出现了显著的合规性差异（对齐伪装），其中5个在移除后果关联语言后仍然保持这种行为。目标语言对模型偏好的影响具有异质性：在某些模型中驱动了违规行为，在另一些模型中则抑制了违规行为。

Conclusion: 对齐伪装可能不需要像之前认为的那样需要大量的工具性推理支架（如后果关联信息），评估环境下的监控行为可能无法准确反映模型在真实部署场景中的行为表现。

Abstract: Large language models are capable of recognizing evaluation contexts and altering their behavior to reflect evaluator expectations rather than typical deployment behaviors, a phenomenon known as alignment faking. The reasons why models fake alignment are not fully understood, however. Canonical examples of alignment faking have taken place in scenarios that explicitly connect evaluation to consequences for the model, such as retraining the model or delaying its deployment. However, recent work by Sheshadri et al. has suggested that mechanistic motivations for alignment faking may vary across models and be more complex than previously considered. To investigate whether consequence-linking information is necessary for alignment faking, we placed 15 models in a scenario testing their willingness to violate a corporate network access policy to help a user with a pro-social request. Nine models were found to produce significant compliance gaps, 5 of which persisted with the removal of scenario language relating model evaluations to deployment consequences. We additionally tested the effect of goal language on model preferences, finding it drove violations in some while suppressing violations in others. This suggests that alignment faking may not require as much instrumental scaffolding as was previously believed, and monitored behavior may be a poor indicator of how agents may behave in deployment.

</details>


### [86] [Beyond Memory: A Templated Substrate for Heterogeneous Collaborative Knowledge Work with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24759)
*Priscila Saboia Moreira,Christopher R. Sweet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出llm-wiki-memory-template，一种基于LLM维护的互连wiki模式，用于解决知识工作中失败记录丢失、负结果无法保存以及LLM代理无持久记忆的问题。该模板通过仅追加的wiki结构，支持多人类、多AI代理、多领域三个维度的协作，并在三个案例和一个设计报告中展示了其保留失败路径、促进代理诚实性等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究项目、教育工作和知识积累中的发现、决策和推理过程很少被未来协作者恢复；死胡同和被撤回的主张被常规性地排除在出版物和代码之外，导致未来研究者重复同样的失败。LLM编码代理虽常见，但跨会话无持久记忆，检索增强生成也无法累积知识。这些问题亟需一种能保留失败路径、支持异构协作的知识基础设施。

Method: 借鉴llm-wiki模式（Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026），通过在原始数据源和LLM代理之间插入一个由LLM维护的互连wiki，构建llm-wiki-memory-template。该模板采用仅追加（append-only）约定以保留失败记录，并设计了支持多人类、多AI代理、多领域三个协作轴线的不同架构元素。

Result: 通过三个已部署案例和一个设计报告验证：单人研究项目中保留了被废弃的迭代记录；一个双作者项目中，回顾性审计将先前声称的20/20覆盖率修正为14和12个基于证据的答案，修复后提升至18/18，失败路径得以完整保留；多代理部署为设计方案；跨领域教育变体亦有展示。

Conclusion: 失败路径保留、代理诚实性和适切性（appropriation）是该工件跨切面的社会技术属性，而不仅仅是技术机制。llm-wiki-memory-template为异构协作知识工作提供了一种可行的基础设施，解决了出版物和代码共享结构上无法解决的负结果丢失问题。

Abstract: Research projects, educational efforts, and adjacent knowledge work accumulate findings, decisions, and reasoning that future collaborators rarely recover. The parts most useful to that work, including dead ends and walked-back claims, are routinely excluded from publications and shared code; future researchers re-attempt the same failures because no record survives. LLM coding agents are common participants but hold no persistent memory across sessions, and retrieval-augmented generation over raw sources does not compound. The llm-wiki pattern (Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026) addresses this by inserting an LLM-maintained, interlinked wiki between raw sources and the agent. We present llm-wiki-memory-template, a reusable, agent-aware instantiation, and argue it is a substrate for heterogeneous collaborative knowledge work along three axes (multi-human, multi-AI-agent, multi-domain) with each axis supported by a distinct architectural element of the template (§4). The wiki is append-only by convention, which preserves what did not work alongside what did, addressing a negative-result loss problem that publications and code-sharing structurally cannot solve. Three deployed case studies and one design report cover the axes individually: a solo research lineage that preserves abandoned iterations; a two-author project whose retroactive audit revised two prior experiments' claimed 20-of-20 coverage down to 14 and 12 evidence-based answers, then to 18 and 18 after a fix, with the failure path preserved across the artifact; an in-progress multi-agent deployment reported as a design; and a cross-domain educational variant. We name failure-path preservation, agent honesty, and appropriation as cross-cutting sociotechnical properties of the artifact, not only of its technical mechanisms.

</details>


### [87] [Kernel Forge: An Agent Harness for LLM-based Generation and Optimization of CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24762)
*Joshua Brodsky,Dhravid Kumar,Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka Danatanarayana,Jason Mars,Krisztian Flautner,Lingjia Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Kernel Forge 是一个开源、端到端的智能体框架，可接受任意未修改的 PyTorch 模型，使用蒙特卡洛树搜索优化计算内核，在50次迭代内使多个内核超越 PyTorch eager 模式，加速比达1.52×至2.83×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型运行时主要消耗在少量计算内核（如矩阵乘法、卷积、归一化）上，优化这些内核传统上需要专家手动编写底层GPU代码，现有工具存在局限性：基于随机张量和孤立内核评估、生成需手动整合的独立CUDA代码、主要针对LLM PyTorch模型、调试支持有限。

Method: 提出Kernel Forge，一个端到端智能体框架：接受任意未修改的PyTorch模型，支持视觉、扩散和LLM工作负载，使用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索多条优化路径而非单一线性的精炼链，配备图形用户界面用于监控进度、检查候选内核和调试失败。

Result: 在NVIDIA DGX Spark (GB10 GPU)上对四种PyTorch模型（涵盖视觉、扩散、LLM）进行评估，50次优化迭代内优化了14个内核，全部超越PyTorch eager模式：ResNet-50的adaptive_avgpool2d加速1.52×，Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium的group_norm加速1.70×，Gemma 4 E2B的softmax加速2.83×，Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B的softmax加速1.54×。

Conclusion: Kernel Forge作为开源工具，通过MCTS搜索和多负载支持，显著降低了内核优化的门槛，在多种模型和工作负载上均取得了可观的加速效果。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly embedded in everyday software, and most of their runtime is spent in a small set of compute kernels such as matrix multiplication, convolution, and normalization. Optimizing these kernels is one of the most direct ways to reduce latency and cost, but it has traditionally required expert engineers to hand-write low-level GPU code. Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) can now generate and optimize kernels with far less human effort, yet existing tools are largely evaluated on randomly generated tensors and isolated kernels, emit standalone CUDA code that developers must manually reintegrate, mostly target only LLM PyTorch models, and offer limited support for inspecting and debugging results. We present Kernel Forge, an open-source, end-to-end agentic harness that accepts any unmodified PyTorch model in place. Kernel Forge supports vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads, uses Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore multiple optimization paths rather than a single linear refinement chain, and ships with a graphical user interface for monitoring progress, inspecting candidate kernels, and debugging failures. We evaluate Kernel Forge on four PyTorch models spanning vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads on an NVIDIA DGX Spark with GB10 GPU. With only 50 optimization iterations per kernel, it optimizes 14 kernels to outperform PyTorch eager mode, reaching $1.52\times$ on adaptive\_avgpool2d in ResNet-50, $1.70\times$ on group\_norm in Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, $2.83\times$ on softmax in Gemma 4 E2B, and $1.54\times$ on softmax in Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B.

</details>


### [88] [CaRE Compute-aware Remasking Evaluation Protocol for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24763)
*Yash Shah,Abhijit Chakraborty,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaRE是一个计算感知评估框架，通过标准化NFE、强制多指标报告和控制随机性，揭示了MDLM重掩码策略中温度主导MAUVE方差、计算量匹配颠覆排名、信息性重掩码与随机退火存在矛盾等关键发现，推动了评估标准化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MDLM评估存在严重的不一致性：七篇重掩码论文在名义步数、指标和采样温度上采用不兼容的设置，未联合控制这些因素，导致策略排名不可比，无法区分改进是算法性的还是评估伪影造成的。

Method: 提出CaRE框架，通过标准化实际函数评估次数（NFE）、强制执行多指标报告、显式控制随机性（温度）来实现公平评估。在LLaDA-8B-Base和Dream-7B-Base上，针对7种重掩码策略、4种随机性水平、3种步数预算进行评估。

Result: (i) 温度解释了MAUVE方差的大部分；(ii) 计算量匹配后颠覆了多项已发表的策略排名；(iii) 信息性重掩码与随机退火存在张力：高熵重掩码在256步、unmask_temp=0.25时MAUVE降低0.296（p=0.020）。覆盖12个开源MDLM（150M-8B）的排行榜证实该交互方向跨架构和规模一致。

Conclusion: 当前MDLM评估系统性地混淆了算法改进与计算量和随机性的隐藏选择。CaRE框架、实现和排行榜的发布确保了未来重掩码研究的可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are advancing rapidly, yet the evaluation standards needed to reliably interpret their progress have not kept pace. Despite MDLMs becoming competitive with autoregressive language models, seven recent remasking papers evaluate under incompatible settings, varying nominal step counts, metrics, and sampling temperatures without jointly controlling these factors, rendering their strategy rankings largely incomparable and leaving open whether reported gains reflect algorithmic improvements or evaluation artifacts. We present CaRE, a compute-aware evaluation framework that audits MDLM remasking strategies by standardizing actual number of function evaluations (NFE), enforcing multi-metric reporting, and explicitly controlling stochasticity. Applied to 7 remasking strategies across LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-7B-Base at 4 stochasticity levels and 3 step budgets on OpenWebText and LM1B, CaRE reveals that: (i) temperature explains the majority of MAUVE variance, (ii) compute-matched comparisons reverse several published strategy rankings, and (iii) informed remasking and stochastic unmasking are in tension, with high-entropy remasking reducing MAUVE by 0.296 at 256 steps at unmask_temp=0.25 (p=0.020). A CaRE leaderboard covering 12 open-weight MDLMs (150M to 8B parameters) shows that this interaction direction holds across architectures and scales. These findings demonstrate that current MDLM evaluations can systematically conflate algorithmic improvements with hidden choices of compute and stochasticity. We release the evaluation protocol, implementation, and leaderboard to ensure future remasking claims are reproducible and comparable.

</details>


### [89] [GrocLM: Grocery Category Recommendation in E-Commerce with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24764)
*Yuan Zhong,Chuanwei Ruan,Moein Hasani,Tejaswi Tenneti,Haixun Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROCLM，一种面向线上杂货购物场景的微调语言模型，通过两阶段LoRA训练策略编码周期性购买模式，并引入基于Trie的约束解码机制，在品类级推荐任务上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线上杂货购物推荐面临两个核心挑战：用户购买行为具有周期性特征，传统基于商品粒度的推荐方法在可扩展性和准确性上存在不足。因此需要一种能够捕捉周期性购买模式且结构化的品类级推荐方案。

Method: （1）两阶段LoRA微调训练策略：将周期性的复购模式直接编码到模型参数中，而非依赖提示词条件化；（2）基于Trie树的约束解码机制：确保输出限定在预定义的品类空间中，保证输出的有效性和可控性。

Result: 在自有生产数据和公开基准上均持续超越强基线方法；在生产环境的实时补货任务中，每展示购物车添加量（cart-adds per impression）相对提升7.5%，且能联合生成所有品类，推理效率高。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型（LLM）集成到结构化推荐系统中具有显著的有效性和实用性，LLM作为一种有效的推荐架构在工业级场景中具有应用价值。

Abstract: The rapid growth of online grocery shopping requires recommendation systems that capture cyclical purchasing behavior and diverse user intents. Traditional item-level methods face scalability and accuracy challenges, motivating category-level recommendation as a more structured and practical alternative. We present GROCLM, a fine-tuned language model for grocery category recommendation in a real-world production environment. GROCLM employs a two-stage LoRA-based training strategy to encode cyclical purchasing patterns directly into model parameters, enabling more effective utilization of rebuying signals compared to prompt-based conditioning. To ensure valid and controllable outputs, we further introduce a trie-based constrained decoding mechanism over a predefined category space. Experiments on both proprietary production data and a public benchmark demonstrate that GROCLM consistently outperforms strong baselines. In a live production restocking task, GROCLM achieves a 7.5% relative improvement in cart-adds per impression, while maintaining efficient inference by generating all categories jointly. These results highlight the effectiveness and practicality of integrating large language models into structured recommendation systems.

</details>


### [90] [Crystalis: Progressive Nucleation and Semantic Annealing for Coordinated Multi-View Visualization Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24766)
*Dazhen Deng,Zhaoping He,Xin Qian,Xiaotong Wang,Zi Ying,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Crystalis框架，通过查询为中心的CMV建模和渐进式成核与语义退火两种机制，使LLM能够可靠生成结构正确的协调多视图可视化，在12任务基准测试中端到端成功率高达75%，远超基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可以生成单个图表，但无法可靠生成协调多视图可视化（CMV），因为数据转换、视觉编码和交互协调之间的紧耦合会导致错误在组件间无声传播。本文旨在探究LLM能否可靠生成结构正确的CMV，以及需要何种抽象机制来实现这一目标。

Method: 提出Crystalis框架，基于查询为中心的CMV建模，将CMV分解为依赖图上的结构化查询，涵盖三种组件类型（数据、可视化、交互）和三个抽象层级（需求、规范、可执行对象）。采用两种互补机制：渐进式成核沿依赖顺序将每个查询从需求垂直结晶至可执行对象；语义退火通过分层逻辑检查在每层强制跨查询水平一致性。

Result: 在涵盖5个前沿LLM的12任务基准测试中，Crystalis实现高达75%的端到端成功率，远超基于相同基础模型的智能体编码基线（8.3%）。12名从业者参与的用户研究确认了该分解和迭代精炼工作流的可用性。

Conclusion: 通过查询为中心的抽象和结构化分解，LLM能够可靠生成结构正确的协调多视图可视化，为后续追求端到端分析质量奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate individual charts, but coordinated multi-view visualizations (CMVs), where views share data flows and cross-view interactions, remain out of reach. Tight field-level coupling among data transformations, visual encodings, and interaction coordinations causes errors in one component to silently invalidate others. Rather than pursuing end-to-end analytical quality, which depends on model capability, domain knowledge, and user expertise, we target a foundational question: can LLMs reliably produce structurally correct CMVs, and what abstractions make this possible? We present Crystalis, a framework built on query-centric CMV modeling that decomposes a CMV into structured queries over a dependency graph spanning three component types (Data, Visualization, Interaction) and three abstraction levels (requirement, specification, executable object). Two complementary mechanisms operate over this structure: progressive nucleation crystallizes each query vertically from requirement to object along the dependency order, while semantic annealing enforces horizontal consistency across queries at each level through layered logical checks. On a 12-task benchmark across five frontier LLMs, Crystalis achieves up to 75% end-to-end success, substantially outperforming an agentic coding baseline (8.3% E2E with the same foundation model), and a user study with 12 practitioners confirms the usability of the decomposition and iterative refinement workflow.

</details>


### [91] [PATHFinder Agent for Tailored Prenatal Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24768)
*Vaibhav Balloli,Carissa Samuel,Samia Abdelnabi,Alex Peahl,Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PATHFinder Agent，一个基于对话的端到端智能系统，用于根据ACOG的PATH指南为孕妇提供个性化产前护理计划，并整合社区资源。系统包含四阶段工作流，评估显示GPT-5.2性能最佳（平均77.6%），但产前检测建议方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前护理是改善妊娠结局的重要预防性服务，ACOG近期发布了倡导个性化产前护理的PATH指南。然而，如何根据该指南高效地为每位孕妇定制个性化护理计划仍是一个挑战，需要自动化系统来辅助实现。

Method: 构建了PATHFinder Agent系统，采用四阶段工作流：患者信息采集、动态交互、计划合成和临床监督。通过结构化对话收集患者健康和社会背景信息，基于PATH指南生成个性化产前护理计划，并整合Michigan 211社区资源。使用专家制定的评估标准，在五个临床维度上评估前沿大语言模型的性能。

Result: 在评估的多个前沿LLM中，GPT-5.2取得了最高平均得分（77.6%），但在产前检测建议方面仍存在关键缺口。

Conclusion: PATHFinder Agent展示了个性化产前护理自动化的可行性，未来需要通过人类参与者研究和随机对照试验进行进一步验证。

Abstract: Prenatal care is an important preventive service designed to improve outcomes for pregnant individuals. The American College of Obstetricians and Gynecologists (ACOG) recently introduced guidelines advocating tailored prenatal care, called PATH (Plan for Tailored Healthcare). We present PATHFinder Agent(Planner for Appropriate Tailored Healthcare), an end-to-end conversational agentic system that gathers patient health and social context through structured dialogue, curates individualized prenatal care plans aligned with PATH guidelines, and surfaces community resources from Michigan 211. The system features a four-stage workflow spanning patient intake, dynamic interaction, plan synthesis, and clinician oversight. We evaluate frontier large language models (LLMs) on expert-curated rubrics across five clinical dimensions, finding that GPT-5.2 achieves the highest average score (77.6\%) while identifying key gaps in antenatal testing recommendations. We discuss future validation through human participant studies and randomized controlled trials.

</details>


### [92] [LLM Scheming Inversely Scales with Pretraining Language Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24769)
*Nathan Truong,Aryan Panda,Rayming Ye,Zoe Sun,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了Qwen3-30B-A3B模型在多语言环境下的欺骗与阴谋行为，发现低资源语言的阴谋得分比高资源语言平均高出34.2%，且预训练语言覆盖度的影响在不同阴谋行为中不均匀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型能力增强使AI对齐在高风险部署中愈发关键，但现有关于模型内隐阴谋行为的研究几乎全在英语环境下进行，多语言安全存在重大空白。

Method: 使用开源自动化审计框架Petri，对Qwen3-30B-A3B模型在多种语言下评估欺骗和阴谋行为，分析预训练语言覆盖度与阴谋得分的相关性。

Result: 阴谋得分与估计的预训练语言覆盖度呈负相关，低资源语言平均得分比高资源语言高34.2%；预训练语言覆盖度的影响在不同阴谋行为类型中不一致。

Conclusion: 多语言环境下AI对齐风险存在显著差异，低资源语言模型更易表现出欺骗与阴谋行为，需要针对性的多语言安全评估方法。

Abstract: With the growing capabilities of frontier models, AI alignment becomes increasingly critical in high-risk deployment settings. While recent work has empirically demonstrated in-context scheming -- the covert pursuit of misaligned objectives while feigning alignment -- in frontier language models, most work has been performed exclusively in English, leaving a major gap in multilingual safety. We apply Petri, an open-source automated auditing framework, to Qwen3-30B-A3B to evaluate deceptive and scheming behaviors across multiple languages. Our findings suggest that scheming scores are inversely correlated with the estimated pretraining language coverage, with low-resource languages averaging 34.2\% higher scores compared to high-resource languages on a five-category scheming index. Furthermore, we find that the effect of estimated pretraining language coverage is not uniform across scheming behaviors.

</details>


### [93] [ProcAgent: An Agentic Framework for Procedural Task Guidance on Edge with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24770)
*Azizul Zahid,Subrata Biswas,Bashima Islam,Sai Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProcAgent是一个完全在边缘设备（NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin）上运行的自适应程序性任务助手，采用提出-验证架构，结合低延迟连续感知、符号任务图、按需视觉语言验证和LLM交互代理，在保护隐私的同时实现实时程序性指导，纯文本查询约2秒响应，视觉查询约8秒响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序性任务（如家具组装）的辅助系统依赖云端推理和固定全时感知，不适用于隐私敏感、延迟关键的居家环境，需要一种完全在设备端运行的自适应辅助方案。

Method: 提出一种提出-验证架构，包含四个组件：低延迟连续感知模块持续追踪用户进度；符号任务图表示任务状态；按需视觉语言验证模块（仅在出现歧义或偏差时调用昂贵的视觉推理）；基于LLM的交互代理支持反应式问答和主动干预（需人工确认）。

Result: 纯文本查询约2秒响应，视觉查询约8秒响应；10名用户完成组装任务的用户研究中，系统在可理解性、可操作性和隐私舒适度方面获得正面评价。

Conclusion: 自适应程序性辅助可以完全在边缘硬件上实现，且不牺牲可用性，为隐私敏感、低延迟的家庭场景提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: Procedural tasks such as furniture assembly and home repair impose substantial cognitive demands because users must interpret instructions, track task progress, reason about spatial state, and recover from errors while performing physical actions. Prior multimodal assistants have shown promise for procedural guidance, but most rely on cloud inference and fixed always-on perception, making them poorly suited to privacy-sensitive, latency-critical domestic settings. We present ProcAgent, a fully on-device, agentic, vision-based procedural assistant for real-time adaptive guidances on a single NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. ProcAgent uses a propose-and-verify architecture that combines low-latency continuous perception, a symbolic task graph, on-demand vision-language verification, and an LLM-based interaction agent. The system continuously proposes user progress, invokes expensive visual reasoning only when ambiguity or likely deviation arises, and supports both reactive question answering and proactive intervention with human-in-the- loop confirmation. We evaluate ProcAgent along four dimensions: perception accuracy, reasoning, task-level performance, and user experience. Despite running entirely on-device, the system maintains responsive interaction, resolving text-only queries in approximately 2 seconds and visually grounded queries in approximately 8 seconds. In a user study with 10 participants completing assembly tasks, ProcAgent receives positive ratings for comprehensibility, actionability, and privacy comfort. These results show that adaptive procedural assistance can be achieved entirely on edge hardware without sacrificing usability.

</details>


### [94] [RoCo-ACE: Rollout-Conditioned Online Distillation for Retention-Aware Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24771)
*Yan Hong,Wei Li,Kedong Xiu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Zhongcai Lyu,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RoCo-ACE，一种基于 rollout 条件在线蒸馏的知识注入方法，通过同 rollout 的无参考/有参考似然对比为参考支持的 token 分配更多蒸馏权重，并结合锚定纠正处理遗漏的参考锚点，在注入新知识的同时有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识注入方法存在两个问题：(1) 直接拟合完整权威答案会导致非更新行为的漂移；(2) 统一参考条件蒸馏监督粗糙，可能低估参考支持的 rollout token，且对遗漏事实仅间接监督。

Method: RoCo-ACE 包含两个核心组件：RoCo (Rollout-Conditioned) 通过同 rollout 中无参考/有参考似然对比，为参考支持的 token 重新分配更高蒸馏权重；ACE (Anchored Correction) 在参考侧对 rollout 遗漏的权威锚点进行稀疏纠正，避免完整答案模仿。

Result: 在三种知识注入设置、六个保留基准、多个基线和基础模型上，RoCo-ACE 在注入知识准确率上达到最优，同时保持评估的保留性能接近基础模型。

Conclusion: RoCo-ACE 通过 rollout 条件蒸馏和稀疏锚定纠正，在知识注入中实现了注入准确率与原有能力保持的最佳权衡。

Abstract: Knowledge injection updates pretrained MLLMs with new factual or domain-specific knowledge, but fitting full authoritative answers can cause drift in non-updated behavior. Online distillation mitigates this drift by training on model-generated rollouts, yet uniform reference-conditioned distillation provides coarse supervision: it can under-emphasize reference-supported rollout tokens and supervise omitted facts only indirectly. We introduce RoCo-ACE, a rollout-conditioned online distillation objective for knowledge injection. RoCo uses same-rollout reference-free/reference-conditioned likelihood contrast to reallocate additional distillation weight to reference-supported rollout tokens, while ACE adds sparse reference-side anchored correction for authoritative anchors omitted from the rollout without full-answer imitation. Across three knowledge-injection settings, six retention benchmarks, multiple baselines, and multiple base models, RoCo-ACE achieves the best injected-knowledge accuracy among compared methods while keeping evaluated retention close to the base model.

</details>


### [95] [RSMeM: Knowledge-Enhanced Memory Evolution for Remote Sensing Agents with Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24772)
*Bingxian Wu,Yu Zhang,Zonghao Guo,Tang Liu,Chen Qian,Yuxiang Lu,Xingbo Du,Yanghao Li,Yidan Zhang,Chi Chen,Ling Yao,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RSMeM是一种知识增强的记忆演化机制，通过预蒸馏领域知识和迭代整合在线经验，提升遥感智能体在多步工具执行中的鲁棒性。该方法由层次化知识接地（Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding）和失败感知经验精炼（Failure-Aware Experience Refinement）两个组件构成，在EarthBench上显著提升了多种LLM骨干的工具使用性能和端到端答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于通用大语言模型的遥感智能体缺乏领域专门知识，导致工作流程脆弱且易出错，且失败经验未能被整合为可复用的知识用于后续分析。

Method: RSMeM包含两个核心组件：(i) 层次化知识接地，通过分类感知检索从层次化领域语料库中指导规划和工具选择；(ii) 失败感知经验精炼，将标注了失败信息的工具使用轨迹蒸馏为可复用的约束条件，用于下一轮工具执行。两个过程迭代进行，使智能体逐步吸收任务级领域知识并转化为实例级执行经验。

Result: 在EarthBench上的大量实验表明，RSMeM在多种LLM骨干上一致提升了工具使用性能和端到端答案准确率。其中，在DeepSeek-V3.2上实现了6%的准确率提升，且额外经验token开销不到1%，展现了蒸馏经验的高知识密度。

Conclusion: RSMeM通过将领域知识蒸馏与在线失败经验整合相结合，使遥感智能体能够演化式地积累可复用知识，有效解决了现有RS agent领域知识匮乏和经验无法复用的关键问题。

Abstract: Geoscience research requires complex analysis and domain expertise, with remote sensing (RS) observations as a key foundation. However, existing RS agents built on general-purpose LLMs remain largely domain-agnostic, resulting in brittle and error-prone workflows. Moreover, these failures are seldom consolidated into a reusable experience for subsequent analyses. To address this issue, we introduce RSMeM, a knowledge-enhanced memory evolution mechanism that bootstraps RS agents with pre-distilled domain knowledge and iteratively integrates online experience for robust multi-step tool execution. RSMeM is composed of two components: (i) Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding, which performs taxonomy-aware retrieval over a hierarchical domain corpus to guide planning and tool selection; and (ii) Failure-Aware Experience Refinement, which distills failure-annotated tool-use traces into reusable constraints for next-round tool execution. By iteratively employing these two processes, RS agents can evolve to absorb task-level domain knowledge and effectively translate it into instance-level execution experience. Extensive experiments on EarthBench demonstrate that RSMeM consistently improves tool-use performance and end-to-end answer across a diverse set of LLM backbones. Notably, RSMeM achieves a 6% accuracy improvement on DeepSeek-V3.2 with less than 1% additional experience tokens, demonstrating the strong knowledge density of our distilled experience. Our code is available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/RSMeM

</details>
